2026-07-12 — Never pick the executor model unilaterally (promoted from per-account memory to repo rule; owner-confirmed): executor+model routing is stamped by `orchestrate` from `tooling/boss/data/rules.md` at plan-authoring time; agy runs use agy's default, Gemini 3.1 Pro (High); a one-off `boss-dispatch.sh --executor/--model` override or a rules.md change is an owner call — surface deviations, don't silently switch. Incident: an orchestrating session once forced Claude onto agy runs during the boss build. Home: `personal-stuff-change-control` non-negotiables.

2026-07-12 — "Beyond state of the art" for this project = autonomy + cost, NOT raw throughput (owner interview): (a) autonomy — the video pipeline running unattended end-to-end, widened from the read-only cron policy one class at a time after the ≥4-clean-weeks gate (~2026-08-08); (b) cost — toward <$10/video all-in (fal-lipsync un-deferral is the primary lever, still owner-DEFERRED). Throughput past 12/mo stays the binding LIVE constraint that gets worked first, but it is table stakes, not the frontier. Homes: `personal-stuff-frontier` (framing), `personal-stuff-video-automation-campaign` (exit horizon).

2026-07-12 — TTS + avatar asset hubs: `pipelines/video/tts/` (was `video/voice/tts-flow/`) and `pipelines/video/heygen/` (consolidates `video/voice/heygen/`, `youtube/open-source/avatar/`, and the registry/manifest formerly in `tooling/cli/heygen-web/`) — owner-chosen convention: each hub holds tracked reference assets (ref voices in `tts/references/` + `REFERENCES.md`; character images/ids in `heygen/characters/` + `registry.json`) and a tracked output manifest (`OUTPUTS.md` / `RENDERS.md`), while generated media lives OUTSIDE the repo in `~/kb-scratch/video/{tts,heygen}/<consuming-pipeline>/` (`_test/` for pipeline-less runs; old `~/kb-scratch/heygen-web-renders` is now a symlink to heygen `_test/`). Hub named `heygen` (owner's literal pick over provider-neutral "avatar") even though the fal-lipsync flow lives inside it. Consumers resolve slugs from the hub and pass paths; they never own voice/avatar assets. The `video/voice/` wrapper is dissolved; heygen-web CLI reads `registry.json`/`RENDERS.md` from the hub (env overrides `HEYGEN_AVATARS`/`HEYGEN_RENDERS_LOG` unchanged). Ref voices copied in from `~/Desktop/my-ref-voices/` (originals left on Desktop).

2026-07-11 — boss: the owner has granted STANDING permission for boss to execute the manual owner-side deploy chain itself — Cloudflare Worker `wrangler secret put`/`deploy`, VPS SSH cron wiring (create `/srv/crons/<job>/`, `.env` from an existing job's copy, crontab install, smoke-test with `timeout` — NOT `gtimeout`, the VPS is Linux), committing wrappers to the `vps-crons` repo (secrets stay `.gitignore`d), and syncing the mirrored `VPS-CRONS.md` copies (repo / `/root` / `vps-crons`). Trigger stays owner-initiated: boss does this only when the owner explicitly says "deploy" (or equivalent) on the item — the deploy gate itself is unchanged, this just lets boss carry it out end-to-end instead of handing the SSH/wrangler steps back. This covers plans whose frontmatter `deploy:` is empty but whose plan body has "Post-merge (owner / SSH-holding session)" steps (e.g. 057/058). Exclusions that still need the human: anything requiring interactive browser OAuth consent (Google token rotation via `setup_auth.py` — `invalid_grant` re-consent), and destructive acts like deleting a live credential/account without an explicit instruction. First exercised 2026-07-11 deploying 057 (cred-probe cron + vps-watchdog Telegram secrets/deploy) and 058 (route-audit pilot cron).

2026-07-08 — boss: dirty-main check is now ENFORCED at dispatch, not just warned at session-start. `boss-dispatch.sh` refuses (exit 1, `--force` to override) if the main checkout has any uncommitted tracked change, printing the offenders, before flipping labels or leasing a worktree; the dirty definition is single-sourced as `boss_repo_dirty()` in `boss-lib.sh` (used by both dispatch and session-start). Why: greenlight parks EVERY merge onto a dirty `REPO_ROOT` as "main checkout busy", and the prior control was a passive reminder (CLAUDE.md session-start step + a warn) that got skipped and silently parked batches twice (2026-07-07 explainer batch, 2026-07-08 PR#12). Moved from "operator remembers to run session-start" to "the dispatch path refuses" so it bites at the point of action regardless of how the session opened. A repo-wide SessionStart hook was rejected: `.claude/settings.json` hooks fire on every personal-stuff session, so auto-running the boss-specific session-start (gh label/PR calls) would misfire on unrelated sessions — the dispatch guard already closes the actual hole.

2026-07-07 — moved `pipelines/video/motion-graphics/Devsplainers/` into `pipelines/youtube/competitor-styles/channels/devsplainers/` (git mv, folder deleted, all internal + cross-file path references updated: `HANDOFF.md`, `hyperframes/SPEC.md`, `pipelines/CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/skills/personal-stuff-video-automation-campaign/SKILL.md`) — reshaped to fit the `channels/<slug>/` schema (`channel.json`, `README.md` → `video-style-dna.md`, `reference-frames/` → `frames/exemplars/`) so it sits alongside `channels/youri/` under one competitor-style-pack convention instead of a one-off location, even though it predates the `fetch_video.py` pipeline (owner call: reshape to the schema AND keep everything, including the non-schema `hyperframes/` build pipeline, nested inside the channel folder rather than left behind). `pipelines/video/motion-graphics/` had no other contents and was removed entirely; historical `plans/006|009|011` intentionally left referencing older paths — records of what was true at the time, not live docs.

2026-07-07 — explainer-videos-pipeline-1: `4-motion-graphics/020-build-graphics` deliberately runs on the Antigravity CLI (agy) at runtime, knowingly overriding the 2026-07-05 Devsplainers PoC finding ("keep Antigravity out of the graphics path" — thin-kit + Antigravity failed quality on all four axes, which is why tutorial-pipeline-2's own 135-build-graphics runs on Sonnet). Owner made the call explicitly after being shown the conflict. Mitigation baked into plan 047: mandatory render + visual inspection before the step is considered done, plus a human review gate (030-review-visuals-human) after. If quality problems recur, that's the signal to revisit this override, not a reason to silently route around it. (plans/047-explainer-pipeline-motion-graphics.md)

2026-07-07 — renamed `pipelines/youtube/kushal-tutorial-pipeline-v2/` to `pipelines/youtube/tutorial-pipeline-2/` (git mv, all internal path references + `pipelines/CLAUDE.md`/`context/bets.md`/still-open `plans/011` updated) and added a new standalone sibling `pipelines/youtube/tutorial-pipeline-1/` (Drive-in → HeyGen spokesperson clips from an existing avatar + the segment's own audio → Drive-out; plan: `plans/043`). Historical `plans/003|012|023` and `plans/runs/20260705-2046-workflow-audit.md` intentionally left referencing the old name — they're records of what ran at the time, not live docs.

2026-07-07 — captain parallel-dispatch plumbing hardened (landed cap/land-plumbing): (a) `cap-spawn.sh` no longer hardcodes `MODEL="sonnet"` — it leaves model empty when `--model` is unset so each lane applies its own default (agy → Gemini 3.1 Pro (High), claude lanes → sonnet); the old default silently ran agy tasks on Sonnet not Gemini; (b) new `bin/cap-land.sh <id>` encapsulates detach-worktree → greenlight → teardown as one command; (c) `.gitattributes` sets `decisions.md merge=union` so parallel branches appending here auto-merge instead of rebase-conflicting; (d) greenlight's "main checkout busy" check uses `--untracked-files=no` so stray untracked artifacts no longer park a land. Convention: crewmates must NOT edit decisions.md — the captain appends after landing. (tooling/captain/)

2026-07-06 — secured yt-claude relay with a per-session token (/token, X-Relay-Token header) and dropped wildcard CORS — prevents CSRF/RCE since non-privileged/attacker scripts cannot read /token due to SOP, whereas Tampermonkey can bypass CORS via GM_xmlhttpRequest.

2026-07-06 — fixed escrow-backup.sh trap to wipe both staging dir and plaintext tarball — referenced "$STAGE.tar.gz" in the trap to avoid unbound variable errors under set -u since STAGE is bound at registration time whereas TARBALL is not yet defined. (infra/escrow/escrow-backup.sh)

2026-07-06 — agy lane v2 after a full capability sweep (subagent-verified): (a) `--add-dir <worktree>` mandatory — agy print mode doesn't bind cwd, and unbound runs roam the home dir under skip-permissions; (b) `--output-format json` (hidden flag) adopted — envelope carries token usage incl. thinking_tokens + conversation_id, so fix-up rounds resume with FULL context via `--conversation <id>` and the captain records real costs; (c) `--print-timeout 180m` (default 5m would kill plan runs); (d) committed `AGENTS.md -> CLAUDE.md` symlink at repo root — agy never reads CLAUDE.md, the symlink bridges every worktree for free; (e) `.agents/hooks.json` + `hooks/secrets-guard.py` — PreToolUse deny on secret paths (infra/secrets, credentials.json, tokens, ~/.gemini, ~/.ssh), live-verified against a real agy attempt — the compensating control for --dangerously-skip-permissions. (tooling/captain/lanes.d/agy-headless.sh)

2026-07-06 — gemini CLI is dead for individual accounts (Google cutoff 2026-06-18, IneligibleTierError — found by the captain's first real scout, tooling/captain/references/antigravity-cli-findings.md): removed the gemini-headless lane + npm package same day it was added. Replaced by `agy` (Antigravity CLI v1.0.16, ~/.local/bin) — same engine and AI Pro sub as the IDE but headless, so it keeps the owner's Antigravity-quota-default decision while deleting the aglock serialization and the GUI-dialog failure class; per-call --model includes Claude Sonnet/Opus 4.6 under the same sub. agy-headless is now the officers' DEFAULT executor; the IDE lane + cap-aglock.sh remain only for explicitly GUI-assisted batches. (tooling/captain/lanes.d/agy-headless.sh)

2026-07-06 — captain v2: worker/officer model (owner hit the 3-parallel-features wall the v1 'orchestrate-in-captain' design serialized). Every unit of work is a crewmate: WORKERS (small clear tasks → greenlight full review) and OFFICERS (one per big feature — own worktree+context, run orchestrate themselves: recon→plans→/plan-review gate→execute→verify; land via greenlight --skip review). Captain = conversation + routing + supervision only; requirements brainstorm stays with the captain (needs the owner), HOW goes to the officer — no duplicate planning, the brief is the handoff. Antigravity = officers' default executor (owner choice, quota economics) behind cap-aglock.sh — a GLOBAL lock that also steers the main checkout onto the executing branch, since one IDE = one workspace. gemini-headless lane added as experimental replacement candidate (real process: PID liveness, no GUI permission dialogs, no lock, fully parallel) — shakedown one real batch before any default flip. (tooling/captain/CLAUDE.md, references/officer-brief-template.md)

2026-07-06 — fixed `tooling/mcp/google-shared/google_auth.py` loading tokens with `Credentials.from_authorized_user_file(path, SCOPES)`, forcing the *current* `SCOPES` constant onto every refresh instead of the scopes the token was actually granted: this broke `gmail-digest` for all 4 accounts (`invalid_scope`) once `gmail.settings.basic` was added to `SCOPES` after those tokens' original consent. Changed to `from_authorized_user_file(path)` (scopes=None → use whatever the token file declares) — same fix already present in `apps/telegram-my-planner/tools/daily-digest/auth.py`, just never ported to the shared helper. Separately, `my-planner`'s vendored VPS token had a dead refresh token (`invalid_grant`, failing silently every day since 2026-06-01) — unrelated to the scope bug, just a stale copy; re-ran `deploy.sh` to re-vendor the live shared `akshatpatidar17` token.

2026-07-06 — built `tooling/cli/notify` as a Telegram-first phone-ping CLI, ntfy kept as fallback: the owner's iPhone doesn't receive ntfy pushes reliably, and `greenlight`/`overnight` need a channel that actually reaches the phone. `notify send` tries Telegram (Bot API `sendMessage`) first, falls back to `pp-ntfy send` only if it's on `PATH` and `NTFY_TOPIC` is set, and otherwise warns to stderr and exits 3 without crashing the caller. Creds live in `infra/secrets/telegram.env` (owner-created via @BotFather + `notify setup`, gitignored) — added a `!infra/secrets/*.example` gitignore exception (had to switch the blanket `secrets/` dir-match to `**/secrets/*` first, since a directory-level ignore can't be re-included per-file) so `telegram.env.example` ships as a template. (tooling/cli/notify/README.md)

2026-07-06 — lifted the no-worktree rule to "managed runs only": agent/executor/parallel runs use the wt pool (tooling/cli/wt, adapted from kunchenguid/treehouse — lease-only, detached HEAD, bootstrap.d symlinks pipelines/.env + credentials.json + .mcp.json); owner interactive sessions, deploys, and skill edits stay on the main checkout — parallel captain/validation runs (plans 034/037) are impossible on one shared tree, and the pool + bootstrap removes the path-keying hazards the old ban protected against. (plans/033)

2026-07-06 — agentic-workflow batch (plans 033–038, from studying Kun Chen's stack in source): build native adaptations rather than adopt his binaries — `wt` (treehouse minus PID-tracking; lease-only + bootstrap.d symlinks), `greenlight` (no-mistakes minus daemon/PRs; fresh-context sonnet review, auto-merge + PUSH origin/main on green, high-risk always parks, `--skip review` for plan-verified work so orchestrate's existing verification is never double-paid), `overnight` (gnhf minus TUI; orchestrator-owns-git, hard caps), `/plan-review` (our skill on lavish-axi transport — the one adopted external), `captain` (firstmate with a pluggable lane registry: dispatch/alive/collect per lane script, propose-and-confirm routing, Antigravity as a first-class cheap lane). Existing skills stay untouched except the change-control worktree rule; orchestrate gains nothing until the batch proves itself. (plans/README.md batch note)

2026-07-06 — hardened pp-yt-transcript against YouTube 429s with (a) a yt-dlp self-update pre-flight (>30 days old → `pip install -U`, one attempt/day via stamp file) and (b) the bgutil-ytdlp-pot-provider PO-token plugin in script mode (clone in `~/kb-scratch/`, symlinked to `~/bgutil-ytdlp-pot-provider` so ALL yt-dlp calls on the Mac get tokens with zero per-caller config) — root cause was 153 unauthenticated transcript fetches from one home IP on a 4-month-stale yt-dlp; script mode chosen over the provider's HTTP-server mode because the CLI is invoked per-call from crons and a persistent launchd service was more moving parts for no gain. Webshare rotating residential proxy (~$1/GB, native youtube-transcript-api support) is the agreed next escalation if 429s persist. (tooling/cli/youtube/README.md "Staying unblocked")

2026-07-06 — lifted the "executor never touches the VPS" rule (plans/README.md): a Claude session holding the `hostinger-vps` SSH key may do VPS wiring (SSH pull + `.env` + crontab) directly instead of handing it back to the owner. The guardrail was written for unattended cheap executors; an owner-directed Opus session operating the VPS is intended and now explicit. Applied immediately to wire the `d1-backup` cron live.

2026-07-06 — nightly D1 backups (`pipelines/backups/d1_export.py` + `vps-crons/d1-backup/` cron): all 5 D1 DBs (clicks-db money, tracker-db team, lists/founders/yt-rankings) exported via the CF D1 export API (polling → nested `result.result.signed_url`) to owned `.sql` dumps in MinIO (`d1-backups/<date>/`, 30-day retention) — CF time-travel only reaches ~30 days and these had no owned backup. Exporter reads CF creds from the process env so the VPS cron carries just 2 keys (no `pipelines/.env` on the box); export path tested Mac-side against all 5 live DBs. VPS wiring (mc install + `.env` + crontab) is the owner follow-up. (docs/skill-library-and-infra-handoff.md Part 2 Tier 1; pipelines/backups/README.md)

2026-07-06 — secrets escrow (`infra/escrow/`): gpg AES256-symmetric archive of every gitignored secret (pipelines/.env + credentials.json, google-shared/ OAuth + 5 tokens, infra/secrets/, apps/*/.dev.vars, ~/.config PayPal), pushed offsite to Drive folder `secrets-escrow` on kushalbakliwal25 — the Mac was the sole copy; Drive is offsite so it survives both Mac AND VPS loss (MinIO-on-VPS would die with the VPS), and collapses the laptop-migration secrets step to "restore one archive". Symmetric + monthly manual run (passphrase typed from the password manager, never stored); asymmetric-key upgrade documented for later cron automation. (docs/skill-library-and-infra-handoff.md Part 2 Tier 1; infra/escrow/README.md)

2026-07-05 — skill scoping rule + memory demotion: single-repo skills live at repo/directory level (`.claude/skills/`, `pipelines/.claude/skills/`), NOT in the account-level store — moved audit-repo-route/dsa-coach/orchestrate to `.claude/skills/`, yt-style-copy + pinterest-* + hyperframes-helper to `pipelines/.claude/skills/`, generate-key-maps to the dashboard-api repo; account store keeps only cross-repo / cwd-independent / account-split skills — repo-level loads for ANY account in the repo and stops taxing unrelated sessions. Per-account auto-memory demoted to scratch cache (promote-to-repo after ~a month, delete duplicates on sight); both accounts' personal-stuff stores consolidated into the repo brain the same day. (docs/skill-library-and-infra-handoff.md Part 4)

2026-07-05 — repo-operating skill library: 15 skills in `.claude/skills/` (repo-map, change-control, cloudflare+vps, deploy, secrets, build-env, debugging, archaeology, diagnostics, validation, docs, hosting, video-automation campaign, idea-to-shipped, frontier) — the departing-steward handoff so any Claude account or a zero-context operator can run the repo; ground-truth verified by 3 review passes. (docs/skill-library-and-infra-handoff.md)

2026-07-05 — orchestrate v2.3 (mined 3 loop-engineering videos): plans/runs/LESSONS.md = cross-run executor-lesson ledger (read at recon, appended after verification — batches compound instead of repeating fix-up rounds); ONE human gate placed pre-dispatch (where a wrong direction invalidates all downstream work), none later; readiness gate checks decisions.md so plans can't propose house-rejected approaches. Deliberately skipped: scheduled self-triggering runs (v3 candidate, autonomy policy still open), approval triage buckets, Stop-hook loops, meta-audits of orchestration assets (YAGNI, single operator).

2026-07-05 — orchestrate v2.2: executor self-fix attempts hard-capped at 5/plan (a busy loop keeps heartbeating, so it looks alive to the watcher while burning budget — cap turns "stuck" into a catchable BLOCKED) + subjective outputs require an explicit rubric in the plan, tier-3 verifier scores against it, never taste — both lifted from agent-loop practice (stop = objective metric OR hard cap; "until satisfied" is not a stop condition).

2026-07-05 — orchestrate v2.1: executor-readiness gate (plan is handoff-ready only when the executor never decides — zero open decisions, tricky snippets authored INTO the plan by the orchestrator) + Difficulty grade routing (tricky → opus subagent; otherwise user's stated executor, unstated → sonnet) + scope check at verify (diff file names ⊆ in-scope) + one-run-at-a-time rule — design/architecture intelligence stays at plan time with Opus; Antigravity's internal model is set in its app UI, not by the skill.

2026-07-04 — dissolved the ty/ theme-folder: renamed to pipelines/ (the shared-Python content/automation workspace, anchored by common/env.py), extracted the redirector + pinterest landing-pages Workers into apps/, and merged ty's decisions/docs/routing into the single root brain — ty/ was the only origin/theme-grouped bucket and forced a two-brain repo; the money-making lens now lives in context/bets.md, not the tree.

2026-07-05 — orchestrate skill v2: automated handoff loop (dispatch → run-log ledger → bg watcher → cheap verify → ≤2 fix-up rounds) with two executors (antigravity via GUI-paste script pinned to "Antigravity IDE"; sonnet as one subagent per plan) — Opus should plan, not re-read executor diffs; only thin signals flow back (exit codes, one-line runlog-status, PASS/FAIL verdicts). Death/BLOCKED = record + surface, no auto-retry (deliberate seam). Runs ledger: plans/runs/<run-id>.md, format in plans/WORKFLOW.md; invariant tests in the skill's scripts/test-runlog.sh. (docs/orchestrator-executor-loop-handoff.md)

2026-07-04 — second-brain context layer scaffolded at context/ (profile/bets/inventory/ideas; pointers not copies; no secrets/PII) — capabilities and cadence existed but identity/goals context did not; sources: planner to-do dumps, income-analysis, hosted-sites index.

2026-07-04 — repo-wide orchestrator→executor convention: expensive model writes self-contained plans into plans/ (template + lifecycle in plans/WORKFLOW.md), cheaper models execute, orchestrator reviews — generalizes the per-model step split already proven in the Devsplainers pipeline.

2026-07-04 — MCP graveyard cleaned: google-calendar-mcp-server, google-docs-mcp-server, elevenlabs removed; gmail-mcp-server + google-shared kept (load-bearing: VPS digest cron + CLI OAuth) with STATUS banners; skill-sync logic extracted to scripts/lib/skill-link.sh with dangling-link pruning + vps-sync flock/pull-failure alert.

2026-07-04 — uniform verification: tsc -b/--noEmit as 'typecheck' script on TS apps, node --check on JS apps; check-apps.sh runs them in CI/local, skipping known-failing lints.

2026-07-04 — media policy: inputs/reference assets are tracked; render outputs are gitignored+untracked (git rm --cached, no history rewrite); heavy generated artifacts (models, work dirs) live outside the repo in ~/kb-scratch/ — the working tree was 18GB and every agent search walked it.

2026-07-04 — ty/ stays a self-governing subtree (own CLAUDE.md/docs/decisions.md); root map delegates into it; Workers live with their domain (redirector stays in ty/workers/) — every external toucher (VPS crons, symlinks, scripts/README.md list) hardcodes current paths, so moves are high-risk/low-benefit; discoverability was the actual problem.

2026-07-04 — placement rule: apps/ = personal products, ty/ = money-making projects, tooling/ = agent surface; every new folder gets README.md + CLAUDE.md from day one.

2026-07-04 — personal-dashboard auth gate restored (session check in requireAuth + client login overlay + startup password-hash self-heal) — the gate had been removed as a no-op while the app was publicly reachable; docs always described it as gated.

2026-07-01 — `apps/lists-app` (lists.agrolloo.com) auth is a stateless signed-cookie password gate (HMAC-SHA256 over the expiry, SESSION_SECRET), NOT Google OAuth + KV like tracker-app. Why: single-user personal app — no per-user identity needed, so a KV session store and OAuth dance are unjustified weight; the cookie is self-verifying with no datastore lookup.

2026-07-01 — Repo routing self-audit ships as an on-demand `/audit-repo-route` skill (personal account), not a VPS Pattern B cron — runs locally over personal-stuff + TY, auto-fixes mechanical map drift (intent-table rows, dead links, missing READMEs) and flags decisions.md staleness. Why: it's manual/Mac-triggered, needs no deploy keys or Telegram, and can edit the working tree directly for review via `git diff`.

- **2026-07-04 — Skill descriptions have a token budget (≤500 chars, hard cap ~700).** Every linked skill's description loads into every session of that account; a skill-hygiene pass trimmed the 12 fattest descriptions (~2.9K chars saved per session) and codified the budget in claude-router v3.1.0. Session mining also produced two new personal-only skills: `scout` (evaluate external tools/ideas against MY stack, verdict-first) and `dsa-coach` (learning/DSA conventions). claude-router's stale `personal-stuff/claude-skills/` paths were fixed to `tooling/claude-skills/` + `scripts/relink.sh`.
- **2026-07-04 — printing-press SKILL.md split into lazy-loaded phase playbooks.** The 294KB monolith (4,484 lines) loaded fully on every invocation; its 13 phase sections were moved verbatim into `references/preflight.md` + `references/phase-*.md`, leaving a 415-line SKILL.md (frontmatter, modes, rules, PII, orientation, run init, a mandatory-read Phase Playbooks map, fast guidance). ~92% cheaper invoke; each phase file is read only when that phase runs. NOTE: this diverges from upstream (mvanhorn/printing-press) — a future upstream skill update will overwrite the split; redo it (script pattern in git history) if that happens.

2026-07-04 — VPS: repointed hyperframes-render card mount from the stale /srv/projects/TY/yt-visuals-hyperframe to /srv/projects/personal-stuff/pipelines/video/card-library and retired the orphaned standalone TY clone + its github-ty deploy key — the TY subtree merge left a second VPS clone the render2 mount still pointed at.
- **2026-07-05 — tutorial pipeline v3: sync by construction, not by editor.** Redesigned kushal-tutorial-pipeline-v2 around a segment map (script block ↔ raw-footage span ↔ VO duration) so assembly is ffmpeg arithmetic and the editor becomes optional QC. Actor routing per step folder suffix: Antigravity for prompt grind, Sonnet-in-Claude-Code where quality is set (script polish, visual plan, graphics via hyperframes skills), deterministic scripts for the rest; Opus/Fable not in the per-video loop. Local-only by owner choice (no VPS). Design: pipelines/youtube/kushal-tutorial-pipeline-v2/SPEC.md; implementation: plans/011. Devsplainers PoC verdict recorded there: thin-kit + Antigravity failed quality on all four axes; keep Antigravity out of the graphics path.
- **2026-07-05 — Competitor style cloning = distill-once style packs.** `pipelines/youtube/competitor-styles/` + the `yt-style` skill: ingest competitor transcripts once via yt-dlp (zero API keys), distill once into a Style DNA + rubric, generate topics/titles/scripts from the DNA only. All LLM work on the Claude subscription. Fine-tuning rejected (20-30 scripts/channel is too little data; weaker local models). RAG rejected for style (global property, not chunk-retrievable) — revisit only as a topic-research index if a pack outgrows context.
- **2026-07-05 — Tracker revamp: person-centric UI over role/stage tabs; definitions stay code.** Focused audit of apps/tracker-app (plans/014-017): the 2026-06-30 PipelineDef engine already delivers "any system, any role breakdown = one typed def", so the revamp is UI-layer only. Freelancer view becomes a single My Work inbox grouped by actionability (needs review / needs action / waiting / up next / done) so a multi-role freelancer never perceives the internal stage breakdown — with a server-side visibility relaxation (canSeeRow drops the gate check; writes stay gated) so assigned-but-not-yet-open stages surface as "up next". Admin gets an attention/exception panel over the matrix instead of a redesign. A system-builder admin UI was rejected: TypeScript-validated defs + deploy is the right cost for a once-a-quarter action. Review history moves to an append-only card_events table because the per-stage feedback slot overwrites each cycle.
- **2026-07-06 — Pipelines-domain skills surfaced at repo root via symlink.** The 6 real skill dirs under `pipelines/.claude/skills/` (hyperframes-helper, pinterest-analyze/board/make-post/research, yt-style-copy) were symlinked into `.claude/skills/` (relative, committed) so any account starting a session at the personal-stuff root sees them — previously they only loaded from inside `pipelines/`, causing `Unknown skill: yt-style-copy` at root. Source of truth stays in pipelines/ (scripts + relative paths unchanged); root just carries symlinks. Not promoted to account-level (relink.sh/manifests) on purpose — that would load them in every repo; this scopes them to personal-stuff. The `.agents/skills`-backed symlinks in pipelines (gsap/hyperframes/etc.) were left alone — already global/cross-repo.
- **2026-07-06 — greenlight gate is deterministic by default; LLM review is opt-in.** The captain's parked `link-clis` land exposed two failures: (1) greenlight's `.result` extractor did a bare `json.load` on the reviewer reply and choked on a ` ```json ` fence — a valid, complete review registered as "missing required keys" and falsely parked; (2) the always-on LLM review scored the WHOLE branch diff vs `main`, so every worker land surfaced findings in unrelated already-landed code, not the crewmate's change. Fix: the land gate is now `rebase → verify → lint → land`, all deterministic — the caller passes `--verify "<cmd>"` (project test cmd + the brief's verify steps; non-zero parks) and syntax lint parks on failure with no LLM fix-round. Deep LLM review became opt-in via `--review` (still parks on high risk / unresolved findings) and belongs to the `improve` skill for advisory audits, not the land path. The fenced-JSON recovery (strip fence + `raw_decode` fallback) was kept regardless. Dead `test`/`docs`/`lint` stage prompts removed; captain worker/officer landing docs updated (workers pass `--verify`, `--review` for risky diffs; doc-only needs neither). Diagnosis started by an agy (Antigravity) session that hit its session limit mid-fix; finished in the captain session (greenlight is captain infra, edited here not by a crewmate).
- **2026-07-06 — Captain trunk = the working branch, graduated to `main`.** Root cause of the scope-bleed above: the captain operated on `advisor/021-workflow-audit`, 20 commits ahead of `main`, while greenlight diffs and lands against `main` — so crewmates branching off the working head looked like they carried all 20 un-merged commits (204-file diffs). Resolved by fast-forwarding `main` to the working branch so `main` is actually trunk; crewmates now branch off `main` and `cap/<id>` vs `main` is only the crewmate's own change. Chosen over adding a `--base` knob to greenlight (keeps the tool assuming `main` = trunk, no extra config surface).
- **2026-07-06 — Captain operating model: crew tests, captain merges (no captain review).** Refines the earlier same-day greenlight decision. Verification lives ENTIRELY in the crew: the captain writes the brief's **Test & fix plan** (what/how to test + how to fix) and the crew runs it, then signals **go-ahead with evidence** (what it tested, results, and for any UI/visual output a rendered frame it actually looked at). The captain does NOT review or re-test — its job is to MERGE correctly (right base/trunk, clean rebase, scope = only this task's diff, main clean, teardown). `greenlight` becomes a pure merge tool: `run --branch cap/<id> --intent "..."` with NO `--verify`/`--review` (rebase → land). Owner picked "trust the go-ahead, just merge" over re-running tests at merge — fastest, full trust in the crew; accepted tradeoff is that breakage introduced by rebasing onto newer main can slip (crew tested on the old base). If a go-ahead turns out wrong, the remedy is a better brief / better crew prompting, not reinstating captain review. Motivating incident: this session the captain landed a card on green *structure asserts* (ids present, emoji stripped) that looked terrible when finally rendered, and did visual fixes inline — both symptoms of the captain trying to verify/do instead of the crew owning it. Corollary now binding: **a UI task's go-ahead requires a render the crew inspected** — "done" without a looked-at frame is not a go-ahead for anything visual. `--verify`/`--review` remain available in greenlight for an explicit one-off ask but are out of the standard loop.
- **2026-07-06 — Hard pre-dispatch confirmation gate (captain must not spawn on its own judgment).** Before running `cap-spawn` for ANY crewmate, the captain posts a one-block dispatch plan — per task: Crew (executor lane + model: Antigravity/`agy`, a CLI lane, `claude-headless`, `claude-tmux`, …), What (1 line), How tested (verification + go-ahead evidence, render for UI) — and STOPS for the owner's explicit go-ahead. No go-ahead → no spawn. A matching `data/rules.md` entry only pre-fills the proposal; it does NOT skip the gate. Batch all tasks of one ask into a single confirmation. Owner correction: the captain kept making routing/dispatch decisions and spawning without asking (e.g. it proposed claude-headless for the video-cards work and immediately spawned instead of waiting) — this gate makes confirmation mandatory, not the soft "propose on novel shape only" it was before.

- 2026-07-07 — Built `boss` (tooling/boss/), a PR-driven implementation orchestrator replacing `captain` (frozen/deprecated). Flow: brainstorm + `orchestrate` make a plan in plans/ (with boss YAML frontmatter), `secretary` raises a PR carrying it, boss reads only the frontmatter, dispatches a crew (claude-p/agy) in a wt worktree, and lands via greenlight. GitHub labels are live state; plans/README.md is the record. Boss shares no code with captain. Spec: docs/specs/2026-07-07-boss-design.md.
- 2026-07-07 — Boss pipeline hardening (post-Fable-review). Fixed the correctness bugs a review surfaced: (1) `claude-p.sh collect` marked a run `done` on a bare `grep '"result"'` — but the `claude -p` JSON envelope carries `"result"` even on `error_max_turns`/`error_during_execution`, so failed/no-work runs got merged. Now it parses `is_error`/`subtype` AND checks HEAD advanced; the HEAD-advanced guard moved to a shared `boss_head_advanced` helper in boss-lib.sh (agy.sh already had it inline; now both share one copy so it can't drift again). (2) **greenlight exits 0 on BOTH land and park** (park() writes state=parked then exit 0) — so `boss-merge.sh`'s `if ! greenlight` was false on a park too, marking parked/verify-failed PRs `boss:done` and pushing a bogus plans/README record. Fix: read greenlight's `$STATE_ROOT/<run>/state` + `parked-reason` files, not the exit code; treat "main checkout busy"/"--no-land" as retryable (leave boss:in-progress, exit 3), everything else as real boss:blocked. Also guard the README-record commit behind an on-`main` check. This greenlight gotcha applies to ANY greenlight caller (captain too). (3) orchestrate Step 3.5 now checks the boss frontmatter (test_cmd/ui/executor/model) is filled — root cause of plans arriving at secretary pre-gapped; executor selection aligned to boss's {claude-p,agy} taxonomy. (4) Ownership resolved: **orchestrate stamps executor+model from rules.md at plan-authoring time; secretary only reads** (spec had wrongly said secretary stamps + "refuses without test_cmd"; corrected to match shipped secretary v1.2.0 + rules.md). (5) boss-session-start now lists gap:* PRs and flags orphaned boss:in-progress PRs (on GitHub, no local state/ meta). (6) _TEMPLATE.md dropped its stale status-block `Executor: antigravity|sonnet|opus` line (frontmatter executor/model is authoritative).
- 2026-07-07 — Boss: the plan registry (`plans/README.md`) is boss-owned on main; plan branches never edit it. Motivating incident: the 044–050 explainer-pipeline batch had every plan branch (and one crew) editing the single shared `plans/README.md`, producing rebase conflicts greenlight parked on and boss had to hand-resolve mid-land (plus a 044/045 plan-number collision between the dossier and explainer batches). Fixes: (1) `boss-dispatch.sh` force-resets the branch's `plans/README.md` to main after merging main (any registry edit on a branch is discarded); (2) crew brief forbids editing it; (3) `secretary` raise stages ONLY the plan file — registry rows go on main directly. The slug-keyed `## boss-landed` list in `plans/README.md` (written on main by boss-merge) stays boss's authoritative landed-record; the status-column cells in the table are advisory/human-maintained (title-keyed, so not safe to auto-flip). Also: `boss-merge.sh` now CLOSES the PR on land (greenlight merges the branch into main directly, so GitHub otherwise leaves it OPEN — "merged but open" was cosmetic-confusing); and `boss-session-start.sh` now warns when the main checkout is dirty (a dirty main silently parks every merge as "main checkout busy" — that blocked this whole batch until pre-existing uncommitted README edits were committed).
- 2026-07-08 — Boss hang/stall hardening (Fable-reviewed) after an `agy` crew ran 83m undetected. Root cause: `alive`=`kill -0 pid` proves the process exists, not that it progresses; the crew wrote a `create_files.py` that generated files by slicing plan-markdown line-ranges, copying ```` ```bash ````/```` ``` ```` fence lines verbatim into `check.sh`, whose leading backtick opened an unterminated command substitution — `bash check.sh` (the plan's test_cmd) blocked forever at 0% CPU, and would have hung `greenlight --verify` at merge too. Fixes, done inline in the boss session (not via the executor pipeline — the orchestrator maintaining its own tooling is the convention's intended exception, and routing a hang-detector fix through the pipeline whose hang-detector was broken is a live risk): (1) every test_cmd runs wrapped in `gtimeout -k 30 <ttl>s` — in the crew brief AND in `boss-merge`'s greenlight `--verify` (test_cmd passed through `printf %q` to survive greenlight's `bash -c`) — so a hang fails fast (exit 124 → park) instead of freezing a run or merge; ttl from optional frontmatter `test_timeout` (default 600s); needs coreutils `gtimeout`, session-start warns if absent. (2) `boss-merge` fence-leak gate: `git diff origin/main...<branch> -- . ':(exclude)*.md' | grep '^+\s*```'` deterministically blocks the land if fence markers leaked into non-md source. (3) `boss_assert_gh` auto-switches the active gh account to `BOSS_GH_USER` on every gh write path (session-start/dispatch/merge/deploy) — a silent flip to the work account had broken every gh call against this private repo mid-session. (4) stall detection in `boss-state`: fingerprint = worktree HEAD + process-tree CPU-seconds + output size; no movement 15m → `STALLED(<n>m)`, 45m → kill the whole tree (→ dead → existing one-fix-up→blocked policy). CPU-tree is the load-bearing signal — executors write their JSON envelope only at the end so output mtime is useless mid-run, and HEAD only moves on commits. Commits: e4ef934 (fixes 1+2+3), 3f61a4c (fix 4).

## 2026-07-09 — timeblock day-planner app
New `apps/timeblock`: personal time-blocking web app. Chose NO Google Calendar sync (self-contained KV store, one JSON blob per day) — the point is speed, not calendar integration. Reused the lists-app stateless signed-cookie auth (house pattern). No frontend build: single static `public/index.html` served via ASSETS. Two-tap block creation (no drag) is the core anti-friction decision.
- 2026-07-11 — **Autonomy policy v1 + first self-triggered run.** Scheduled/unattended agent runs are READ-ONLY — they may read the repo and send reports/alerts (Telegram), never edit, commit, push, or deploy; anything that writes stays owner-initiated. Pilot: weekly `route-audit` cron (VPS, `claude -p`, report-only adaptation of the `audit-repo-route` skill; write tools disallowed at the flag level, prompt enforces REPORT-ONLY, VPS clone has a read-only deploy key). Widen only after ≥4 clean weeks, one class at a time. (plans/058, infra/route-audit/). Deployed via boss on 2026-07-11 alongside the 057 cred-probe cron + vps-watchdog Telegram-alert deploy.
- 2026-07-10 — **One writer per checkout: branch-guard hook + merge-only landings.** Session-transcript autopsy of the 054/055 tangle: two live sessions shared the main checkout, one switched branches under the other, so timeblock commits (cd6314a, 479496e) landed interleaved on `advisor/055-heygen-web-layered-architecture`; landing then required exclusion-cherry-picks onto fresh branches (40+ conflicts, duplicate same-message/different-SHA commits on main, TWO competing timeblock squashes — local 27728e2 vs origin f2eba0e). Fixes: (1) `.claude/hooks/branch-guard.sh` (PreToolUse, wired in `.claude/settings.json`) blocks `git switch`/`git checkout <branch>` in the MAIN checkout when another session's transcript was written <5 min ago — path restores, worktrees, and solo work are untouched; deliberate override = `GUARD_OK=1` prefix. (2) Rule: at most ONE active session uses the main checkout; every additional concurrent session works in a `wt` worktree on a real named branch (`git switch -c task/<name>` inside it), never detached HEAD for work that must land. (3) Landing = merge the task branch into main; NEVER cherry-pick a subset off a mixed branch — if a branch got mixed, the failure already happened at isolation time. (4) Shared registries (plans/README.md, decisions.md, my-hosted-sites.md, INFRA.md) are the conflict magnets: update them on main at landing time only (boss already enforces this for plans/README.md). Cleanup done same day: main reset to the origin squash (superset), 33 stale local branches deleted, all task content verified landed before deletion.
- 2026-07-12 — RVC-flow archived to `pipelines/archive/rvc-flow/` (was `pipelines/video/voice/RVC-flow/`). Superseded by `tts-flow/`'s IndexTTS-2-on-Modal engine, which regenerates speech from corrected text instead of skinning the source recording's flaws (RVC structurally can't fix delivery/pronunciation — see tts-flow/CLAUDE.md "Why this exists"). Kept, not deleted, in case a free/local voice-skin fallback is needed again. Updated pointers: pipelines/CLAUDE.md folder map, pipelines/video/voice/README.md, tts-flow/CLAUDE.md's `../RVC-flow/` reference, personal-stuff-repo-map's stale-path note.
- 2026-07-12 — **Final workflow is VO-first (sync by construction) — dub-flow sync work retired.** Owner confirmed the multi-channel final workflow (pipelines/youtube/final-workflow/): the processor generates the TTS voiceover FIRST and the tutorial maker records the screen while listening to it — SYNC-PROBLEM.md's option C. This answers the Phase-0 gate in personal-stuff-video-automation-campaign: the dub-flow per-sentence sync upgrade (campaign Phase 2) is NOT needed for this workflow; the video/tts anchor+silence-absorb path stays only as a legacy fallback for dubbing pre-existing recordings. Also confirmed same session: TTS cost is ~$0.50/video (IndexTTS-2 on Modal; the $2 in the notes was a stale placeholder), the $150/mo reviewer covers SCRIPT review only (final-video QC is an open gap), and Category 1 channels (big review/comparison) share this same screen-recording workflow.
- 2026-07-12 — **Avatar path stays HeyGen for now; fal-lipsync deferred.** Owner decision: the final workflow keeps HeyGen ($1/min, ~$5/video at 5 min) as the avatar generator; the validated fal-lipsync replacement (Kling base loop + LatentSync, ~$0.30–0.40/min, gaze-down pose) is deferred — do not push the migration or build the avatar CLI until the owner revisits. Cost model in pipelines/youtube/final-workflow/final-workflow-notes.md updated accordingly (~$48/video at 12/mo).
- 2026-07-17 — **Final-workflow motion graphics: beat-synced HyperFrames cards, one LLM call per video.** Design spec: `docs/specs/2026-07-17-motion-graphics-beat-sync-design.md`. Key calls (owner-confirmed in brainstorm): (1) HyperFrames over Remotion — one HTML file is both the live storyboard preview and the render input, so the preview IS the final graphic (Remotion's build step would split them); (2) cue detection from MP3/transcript only, never video frames (VO-first makes the MP3 the master clock); (3) the per-video LLM step emits cues.json quoting transcript ANCHOR PHRASES, never timestamps — a deterministic resolver maps phrases to Whisper word timestamps, so progressive reveals (e.g. each pro/con) land exactly when the VO speaks them; (4) card contract extended: beats/timing become DATA next to content, timelines are built from `items[].at` — new-card hard requirement in the step-135 rulebook; (5) cue-pass executor is pluggable (Sonnet default; Antigravity allowed ONLY for form-filling or card-authoring under the recorded 2026-07-07 render+inspect mitigation — the graphics fence stands); (6) local storyboard server (card-gallery serve.mjs pattern) with per-cue MP3 slices driving the composition clock; (7) seed ~8-10 beat-parameterized core cards up front; (8) rendered clips are video-only, editor aligns via manifest timecode.
- 2026-07-17 — **Graphics flow gets its own step-wise pipeline folder; card-library stays the asset hub.** Owner call after 062–065 landed: the beat-sync flow moves from `card-library/flow/` to `pipelines/video/graphics-flow/` structured like tutorial-pipeline-1 (`steps/NNN-*`, `lib/`, `scripts/check.sh`, `PIPELINE.md`). Per-video data lives IN the pipeline folder at `videos/<slug>/` — text artifacts (transcript.json, cues.json, resolved.json, manifest.md) are COMMITTED for reviewable history; media (vo.mp3, renders/, slices/) gitignored, honoring the no-generated-media-in-git rule. Cards + catalog.json + gallery stay in card-library (hub-vs-flow split, same as tts/heygen). manifest.md moves to the workdir root since renders/ is ignored. (plan 066)
- 2026-07-17 — **Graphics-flow hardening (owner-directed, done inline, not via boss).** Critique pass after 062–066 found real gaps; fixes landed same day: (1) per-card capacity limits — `max_beats` + `max_reveal_chars` on all 8 beat cards in catalog.json (conservative estimates from layout math, unverified until video #1; beat-smoke enforces the fields; rulebook + prompt treat them as hard limits, split-or-trim on overflow); (2) `card-library/DESIGN.md` — the library's implicit design system (burnt-amber bg #3a1f08→#0a0805, orange accent #fb923c, green/rose semantics, gold for wins, Inter) distilled into the contract new cards must meet (the devsplainers pack is a DIFFERENT aesthetic — the library has its own); (3) timeline `offset` field in cues.json → manifest "place at" column, fixing the silent VO-starts-at-00:00 assumption; (4) step 010 accepts vo.mp4/mov/mkv/m4a/wav and ffmpeg-extracts vo.mp3. Known accepted risks: retrofitted cards never visually audited (owner chose to find issues on the board during video #1), density defaults uncalibrated until real videos tune the rulebook.
- 2026-07-18 — **Graphics-flow model routing (owner-confirmed).** Cue pass (step 020): Sonnet default, agy/Gemini allowed as the free alternate to trial after video #1. Novel-card authoring: **Opus** (owner call — quality-setting library growth, occasional, not the per-video loop, so the 2026-07-05 Opus-out-of-the-loop rule doesn't apply; Antigravity still only under the 2026-07-07 render+inspect mitigation). Everything else in the flow is model-free scripts. Opus supervises only the first dry run(s); steady state needs no Opus per video.
- 2026-07-18 — **Boss executor default flipped to agy.** Owner decision: `tooling/boss/data/rules.md` default is now agy (Gemini 3.1 Pro High, Antigravity sub — effectively free tokens) for any plan passing the fully-inlined bar; claude-p is the exception (sonnet: taste-judged content or not-fully-inlinable plans; opus: tricky). Grounds: agy's LESSONS track record (055 refactor, 054 greenfield, 2026-07-18 card batch — all first-try green under fully-inlined plans). Riders preserved: render+visual-inspection gate on graphics output (2026-07-07 mitigation), verify by commits never envelopes, fully-inlined plans are a hard prerequisite not a preference. Plan 067 re-stamped agy on its PR branch (#24). orchestrate SKILL.md defaults text aligned.
- 2026-07-18 — **Boss `ui:true` screenshot gate removed.** Owner decision: plans no longer trigger a crew screenshot step. The mechanism made the crew render a view, commit the PNG to `plans/runs/evidence/`, and post it as a PR comment — but the sole owner never reviews those artifacts and there is no second reviewer, so it was produced-and-never-consumed. It was also never a real merge gate (boss-merge's hard gates are test_cmd + fence-leak + lint; the screenshot was only a crew-brief instruction plus a manual eyeball). Net effect was pure cost, e.g. the PR#31 (074-overflow-qc) max-turns fix-up burned a whole extra claude-p dispatch (~$4) purely to render an image nobody looks at, on code that was already green. Removed the `ui:true` block from `tooling/boss/bin/boss-dispatch.sh`'s crew brief and dropped the now-dead `ui=` frontmatter read. Visual changes are eyeballed locally on demand (e.g. `node pipelines/video/graphics-flow/lib/board.mjs <slug>`). Applied mid-batch before PR#33 (076) so the last `ui:true` plan didn't repeat the waste. Follow-up (not done): the orchestrate skill still emits a `ui:` field in plan frontmatter — harmless (ignored) but can be dropped from the skill source in tooling/claude-skills/ later.
- 2026-07-18 (graphics-flow, owner): **structural consistency beats the repetition cap** — cards filling the same semantic slot once per compared item (section openers) MUST be the same card for every item; such cards carry `structural: true` in catalog.json and are exempt from the 3×-per-video cap (the cap had forced asymmetric openers on test-01 v2, the wrong tradeoff). Lint enforces the exemption.
- 2026-07-18 (graphics-flow, owner): **tool logos always render muted** (saturate .5, brightness .95, opacity .9, small relative to text) — full-color brand marks inject hues outside the card palette. Rule lives in card-library/DESIGN.md "Tool logos"; applied across all logo-consuming cards.
- 2026-07-18 (graphics-flow, owner): **no per-tool pricing graphics** during tool segments (the pricing page is on screen); ONE consolidated pricing comparison in the final comparison section. Companion rule: no graphic over step narration the screen recording shows (step-flow is for off-screen processes only). Both in RULEBOOK + prompt.
- 2026-07-18 — **Renamed pipelines/video/graphics-flow → visuals-flow:** the folder now owns the avatar/screen-recording shot plan (GFX-07) as well as graphics, per docs/specs/2026-07-18-avatar-shot-plan-design.md. Old name in historical docs/plans left as-is.
- 2026-07-18 (visuals-flow, owner): **Final-video assembly is a pipeline step (090), planned as 082.** Owner decisions from the design Q&A: the screen recording is already VO-aligned (recorded live by the tutorial maker) so assembly is fully deterministic; audio = vo.mp3 throughout (screen + avatar clips muted — avatar clips were lipsynced to VO slices anyway); hard cuts only; final.mp4 is BOTH a shippable output and an editor draft — the editor handoff bundle stays unchanged, per video the owner picks which route ships. Conventions set: screen recording lives at `videos/<slug>/screen.mp4` (owner-provided, gitignored), final.mp4 goes to `~/kb-scratch/video/visuals-flow/<slug>/` (env `ASSEMBLE_MEDIA_ROOT`), committed `assembly.md` is the EDL. Assembly builds the pure VO timeline and ignores `offset`. Deferred with seams noted in the plan: corner-track compositing, non-aligned screen (tutorial-pipeline-2 future), BGM bed.
- 2026-07-18 (visuals-flow, owner): **Assembly speed pass approved (plan 083).** Rework agreed in brainstorm: composite overlays inside the intersecting segment encodes so the final pass becomes a stream-copy concat + audio remux (one encode per frame instead of two); auto-detect h264_videotoolbox with libx264 fallback (`--encoder` override, tests always force x264 since the VPS has no videotoolbox); `--draft` = 720p preview writing final-draft.mp4 (never clobbers final.mp4). Quality bar is "clean at generous bitrate" because YouTube re-encodes uploads. Rejected: parallel segment encodes (moot with one HW encoder unit), cloud rendering (upload cost), keyframe-aligned smart-render (fragile; overlay-in-segment achieves it cleanly).
- 2026-07-19 — visuals-flow effects layer: assembly effects are pluggable `lib/effects/` modules + per-video `effects.json` manifest (owner-editable per instance, merged by id) + `EFFECTS.md` rulebook (rule surface #7); `assemble.mjs` is a host — adding an effect never edits the encode loop (plan 093). Reference-video effect discovery is automated via `analyze reference <url>` (plan 094; scene-score + luma-spike detectors, contact-sheet forensics).
- 2026-07-19 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Youri style clone: grammar + density adopted from reference analysis.** Three reference videos (Youri van Hofwegen) frame-analyzed via `analyze reference` into committed reports (`pipelines/video/visuals-flow/references/`); owner-approved design `docs/specs/2026-07-19-mode-structure-density-design.md`. Owner decisions: one rule surface for all video lengths (per-minute cadences, never per-video counts); density STARTS at the reference calibration (fullframe beat per 45–90s, overlays ≤3/rolling-60s, cold-open + end-card allowances) — supersedes "wait for fold feedback" for the starting numbers, the 060 fold still tunes from there; corner avatar bubble UN-DEFERRED with the reference look (circle, orange ring, screen segments only); TH asymmetry adopted (hard cut INTO host, wipe out; no punch-ins on spans <45s) — explicitly owner-vetoable at the next draft watch. Landed as plans 095–101 (flash wipe, keyword captions, verdict/score overlay cards, progressive table + headline-chips slate, density recalibration, bubble module, TH polish). Canvas "Level 2" (windowed screen on gradient) deliberately parked as GFX-16: prototype on ONE real video before any grammar commitment.
- 2026-07-19 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Parallel segment encodes UN-REJECTED (reverses part of the 2026-07-18 plan-083 decision).** The 083-era rejection ("moot with one HW encoder unit") predates the effects layer; captions/drift/wipe filter graphs made segments CPU-bound, so drafts regressed 4m23s → 15+ min and parallelism pays again. Owner picked the local speed pass (plan 102/GFX-17: --jobs pool, content-keyed segment cache, libass ASS captions replacing the PNG-per-chunk pipeline, --bare tier). Cloud rendering stays rejected FOR SPEED but is re-framed as a portability/async play: VPS async assemble parked as GFX-18 with the precondition that screen recordings already land in Drive; decide after 102 ships.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Effects get a THIRD approval gate + a board lane scoped as a timing preview (spec `docs/specs/2026-07-20-board-effects-lane-design.md`, plan 104).** Owner decisions from the brainstorm: (1) approvals stay per-artifact (cues → render/shot-pass, shots → avatar render, effects → assembly) because they gate different steps at different times — one combined approval can't work mechanically; `assemble.mjs` refuses an unapproved `effects.json` with the same `--force` semantics as the other gates, and any board-side effects change resets approval. (2) The lane's playback preview is CSS-simulated and explicitly scoped to timing/density/selection — NOT final pixels — because each effect's look is a global module constant already frame-verified against references, while effects.json only controls where/when/whether instances fire; captions preview is fully honest (reuses the real planCaptions/markKeyword). Per-instance ffmpeg micro-previews rejected (duplicate the draft render slowly; --draft is the doubt escape hatch since plan 102). (3) Board-writable fields: `enabled` per instance + `approved` only; params stay JSON-file edits. Deferred by owner same session: GFX-05 board edit affordances, GFX-08 full play-through (104's master player is its seam), GFX-11 agy cue-pass trial (waits for video #2).
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow) — **Unit suites must never render or touch committed per-video data; check.sh's explicit list is the only test registration point.** Found during the 104/105 brainstorm: `lib/effects.test.mjs` existed but was never wired into check.sh, and as written it deleted/rewrote the real `videos/test-01/effects.json` and spawned a full `assemble --draft` (multi-minute; it clobbered the kb-scratch final-draft.mp4 when run by hand on 2026-07-20 — restored from git + a clean re-assemble). Plan 105 rewrites it against a temp fixture workdir with no assembly spawn and wires it in. Corollary recorded in that plan: a test file not in check.sh's list silently never runs.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow) — **Plan 102 speed pass benchmarked on test-01: full 32-min draft in 3m16s wall on a COLD segment cache** (65 segments encoded, `--jobs` 3), vs 15+ min pre-102 and 4m23s pre-effects. Warm re-drafts will be faster (cache now populated). GFX-18 (VPS async assemble) was parked pending this number — it can be decided whenever portability/async actually matters; speed alone no longer justifies it.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **GFX-16 windowed-canvas resolved as a recording-side convention, not a pipeline build.** The Level 2 look (screen recording as a rounded title-barred floating window drifting on a brand-gradient canvas, per `references/-vwHldNaGPI.md`) will be produced by the tutorial maker at record time (Screen-Studio-style tooling), so screen.mp4 arrives with the treatment baked in and assembly treats it as ordinary footage. Trade-off accepted: the look is committed at record time (no post-hoc scale/palette tweaks, no A/B without re-export). Riders: (1) the maker's FIRST sample video in the style runs through the pipeline and gates adopting the format — the 2026-07-19 one-video-prototype gate moved upstream, not dropped; (2) `drift` gets a per-video kill in effects.json for windowed footage (double-motion otherwise); caption y-position gets eyeballed on the sample; (3) the maker gets a small style kit — brand gradient values + "title bar names the app shown" — so the recorded canvas doesn't clash with the card library palette. No visuals-flow code changes.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Shot-plan cadence adopts the Youri reference RHYTHM; the total full-screen budget stays frozen.** The 2026-07-19 style-clone wave recalibrated graphics density but left shot numbers at the 2026-07-18 cost-driven calibration; owner now split the concerns: totals unchanged (AVATAR_FULL_CAP 300s / TARGET 240s scaled — they encode HeyGen ~$1/min production cost, ≈$5/video; the reference's measured 25-30% full-screen TH is explicitly NOT adopted), but within that budget the rhythm becomes the reference's fast host↔content cycle: SPAN_MIN 12→10s, span-length warning split (mid-video bridges warn >45s, intro/outro spans warn >120s; was flat 150s), cadence gap warning 300→180s, 070 prompt/RULEBOOK rewritten to "10–30s bridges, cycle fast and regular". Planned as 106; the 060 fold tunes from this starting point (same relationship plan 099 had to cue density).
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Corner bubble retuned to the Youri reference after the test-02 draft read as "a rectangle with a small frozen circle in it".** Three separate defects, all in `lib/effects/bubble.mjs`: (1) the ring+glow was drawn on a canvas exactly `DxD`, so the ring sat on the canvas edge and `gblur` was clipped by it — that clipped blur, not the alpha mask, is the "rectangle"; fixed by drawing on a padded canvas (`S = D + 2*PAD`, `PAD >= 3*GLOW`) composited at `OX-PAD`/`OY-PAD`. (2) The bubble was half its reference size: `references/vPqSgj8Ta3Y.md` records "~120px", but that was measured on a ~480-tall frame and got transcribed into `BUBBLE_D_1080` as if it were a 1080 value — remeasured at ~28% of frame height, so D 150→300, INSET 40→24. (3) The rotating arc (plan 103) was invisible, not absent — `RING_PX_1080: 3` under `sigma: 6` is mush; ring 3→10px, glow sigma 6→3 and now scaled by canvas height like every other constant (`GLOW_SIGMA`→`GLOW_SIGMA_1080`), so 720p draft and 1080p final finally match as the module always claimed. Owner chose reference-matched sizing and keeping the rotation; tighter face-crop framing inside the circle (reference is head-and-shoulders, ours is full-torso because `scale=D:D` fits the whole 1080 source height) deferred. Also found in passing: the ring's `color` source had no `d=`, so it was an unbounded source — harmless inside assemble.mjs only because it caps each segment's output duration, but it made a standalone 20s render take >600s vs 6.7s once bounded.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Second bubble pass: measured the ring off a HIGH-ZOOM reference still instead of the contact sheet, which reversed part of the first pass.** Contact-sheet tiles (480x270) blur a hairline ring into something that measures ~10px at 1080; on a zoomed still (circle d=432px) the ring core is ~7.5px FWHM = 1.7% of D, i.e. **5px at D=300** — so `RING_PX_1080` went 3→10→5, and the original 3 was closer to right than the first correction. Lesson: never measure a fine feature off the moment contact sheets; pull a zoomed frame. Three further gaps closed: (1) the ring was one `geq` blurred by `GLOW`, which smears the core — now `split` into a crisp core (`CORE=1` anti-alias) over a `GLOW`-blurred bloom, so it reads as a hairline with a halo, not a fuzzy band. (2) The gleam used `pow(cos,2)`, a gradient washing over half the circumference; the reference bloom is localised to ~±40° with a blown-out flat top, so it's now `min(1, pow(...)^6 * 2.5)` — the clamp supplies the plateau. (3) Framing un-deferred and fixed: fitting the full source height put the head at ~58% of the circle with a band of torso below, vs ~73% (portrait) in the reference — added `AVATAR_ZOOM` 1.3 + `AVATAR_FOCUS_X/Y` focus-point crop, tuned on `specs-man` and explicitly per-template. STILL DELIBERATELY DIFFERENT: the ring is brand orange, the reference is neon green — the recolor is the standing decision from the style-clone wave, not a gap.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Third bubble pass: the gleam is a SEPARATE, WIDER layer, not a colour shift inside the ring.** Owner rejected pass 2 with the actual structural insight ("reference had thin circle and while moving motion is curvier thick around thin circle"). Verified by taking radial luma profiles off the high-zoom still at the gleam angle vs away from it — they are different SHAPES: away from the gleam a sharp ~5px-FWHM spike that drops straight to background with NO halo; at the gleam a blown-white ~12px-FWHM plateau reaching ~22px. So the ring is (1) a thin constant-colour hairline with no glow of its own, plus (2) a travelling bloom ~2x wider, near-white, whose ALPHA is angularly shaped then blurred. Both earlier attempts modelled the gleam as a colour mix inside a fixed-width band, which keeps the ring a uniform width and therefore cannot read as motion around it — no amount of tuning sharpness/contrast fixes that, it needed the second layer. Constants: RING_PX_1080 5→4, new GLEAM_PX_1080 8 / GLEAM_COLOR / GLEAM_SIGMA_1080 4, RING_SPIN_SHARP 6→8; GLOW_SIGMA_1080 retired (the hairline has no halo). Method lesson worth keeping: when a look is "close but wrong", profile the reference along the axis the eye is complaining about rather than tuning scalars — averaging the radial profile over ALL angles is what hid this for two passes.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Fourth bubble pass: the ring WIDTH RATIO is the metric, and ring constants are calibrated by measurement, not derived.** Pass 3 had the right two-layer structure but still looked wrong; measuring the render (not the code) showed why: hairline 2.65% of D against a 1.16% reference (2.3x too thick) while the gleam was already fine at 1.1x, so the gleam/hairline ratio was **1.19x against the reference's 2.40x** — the bloom cannot read as "thick around thin" when the base is not thin. Two mechanical causes: (a) the band expression `between(hypot, R-RING-1, R+1)` is `RING+2` wide, not `RING` — a fudge inherited from the original module, now centred on R at exactly the nominal width; (b) ring/blur widths were rounded to whole pixels, and a 1px rounding on a 2px hairline is a 50% error at 720p, which is the resolution actually being eyeballed — they are fractional now. Final calibration measured 1.00%/2.50%/**2.50x ratio** vs reference 1.16%/2.78%/2.40x. Method decision: these constants are NOT derivable on paper (a closed-form gaussian FWHM materially overestimates what `gblur ... steps=2` renders — trusting it is what produced a 48%-too-wide gleam mid-pass), so `scripts/measure-bubble-ring.py` is checked in and is the calibration loop: render, measure, adjust. Corollary for the unit test: it asserts the structural invariant (gleam wider than hairline in both band and blur, spread ratio >= 1.8x) and explicitly does NOT assert rendered widths from a formula, because that formula disagrees with the renderer.
- 2026-07-20 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Post-assembly human touch-up loop: DaVinci Resolve integration is a deterministic FCPXML EXPORT (plan 109), never MCP-driven editing; plus a filmstrip QC pass (plan 110).** Owner motivation: after Claude assembles, the remaining fixes (graphic too short, timing off, remove a card) are faster as drags in an NLE than as chat round-trips. Decisions: (1) exporter generates `timeline.fcpxml` + media clips — headless, unit-testable, imports into Resolve free edition AND Premiere; the vendored `davinci-resolve(-advanced)` MCP servers (375 tools) stay ad-hoc/opt-in, not load-bearing — the fat-MCP settled battle and the one-LLM-call-per-pass architecture both stand. (2) Timeline design: V1 = effect-baked overlay-free segments (WYSIWYG vs final.mp4; effects/captions are paint inside clips, not Resolve objects — effect tweaks stay effects.json + re-assemble), overlay graphics ride their own lane as individually movable clips, VO is the master audio track; `--bundle` produces the portable remote-editor folder. (3) Reverse timeline-diff (edited FCPXML back → fold items) DEFERRED until the first real edit round-trip proves the workflow. (4) browser-use/video-use NOT adopted (general-purpose chat-editor would reset the fold-loop's accumulated judgment); only its self-validation idea is borrowed — plan 110 builds contact sheets + waveform per EXPECTED event (from assembly.md/effects.json, no scene detection needed) and a session scores them into a committed qc-report.md, answering GFX-10 / final-workflow open problem #3's "cheap mechanism" bar.
- 2026-07-21 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Editor handoff goes NATIVE: layered FCPXML + FX overlay clips (Phase 1); .drp/DRT authoring (Phase 2) validated by POC but DEFERRED.** Owner rejected the baked-segment editing story after first hands-on Resolve session ("I want everything visible in the timeline like an editor built it — copy/edit each thing"). Decisions: (1) new default export = layered native project — continuous screen.mp4 on the spine, avatar/graphics/overlays on their own lanes, VO underneath, captions as sidecar SRT, and EVERY renderable effect instance as a pre-rendered transparent ProRes clip on an FX lane (native copyable objects; this design holds in Phase 2 too since Resolve has no flash-wipe transition); baked mode stays as `--baked` for WYSIWYG ship checks. (2) Transform-class effects (punch-in, Ken Burns, blur-whip displacement) are dropped from the editor export with a marker each — Resolve-native equivalents exist; ship path unaffected. (3) DRT POC verdict (drt-poc/, Resolve 21.0.2 free): structure/transitions/generators author offline and import as native objects ✓; media-pool items and Text+ comps arrive broken ✗ — both are cloned zstd/binary blobs that don't survive machine drift. Phase 2 preconditions recorded in docs/specs/2026-07-21-native-editor-export-design.md; tracked as GFX-19. Spec is the single design record; plans 111 (FX clip renderer) + 112 (native FCPXML mode).
- 2026-07-21 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Motion-graphics density recalibrated ~2× UP, and the "leave demos bare" stance reversed to "punctuate demos".** Owner watched test-01 and flagged long bare stretches (measured: 18 cues / 32min = 0.56/min, with interior gaps up to 7.4 min of raw recording). Root cause was by-design, not a bug: the cue-pass rulebook's "default state is NO graphic / when in doubt skip / never a graphic over demo-step-narration" stance (itself a 2026-07-18 fold, test-01 c06/c09/c15) blanked out the demo stretches that are most of these videos, and the lint floor sat at 0.55/min. Owner picked **moderate (~2×)** density and **punctuate demos** (over "keep demos clean"). Changes, made inline (not via boss — owner chose inline): `lib/lint-cues.mjs` constants `GAP_FULLFRAME_MAX` 90→60, `GAP_FULLFRAME_MIN` 45→35, `TARGET_RATE_MIN` 0.55→1.0, `TARGET_RATE_MAX` 1.3→1.9, plus a NEW **W6 bare-stretch** warning (no interior gap >50s between any two cues, cold-open/end-zone exempt) — the direct guard against the bare stretches. The rulebook + prompt (edited together) flip the philosophy: graphics are a near-constant presence; demos are PUNCTUATED with the spoken layer (keyword-statement/kinetic-sentence of the point, lower-third/callout naming the feature, tool-icon at tool switches) but still NOT a redundant step-flow over a visible click (that half of the old fold stands). This supersedes the Youri-wave starting density numbers by the same owner-directive precedent (2026-07-19); the 060 fold tunes from here. Also added when-to-fire rules for the 4 new cards from the 2026-07-21 pack (plan 114: prompt-typing, tool-glass-tile, keyword-statement, icon-pills). NOT yet re-run on test-01 — the recalibration changes the rules; applying them is a fresh cue pass.
- 2026-07-23 (tutorials, owner) — **Tutorial production goes VO-first; tutorial-pipeline-2's dub-sync machinery rejected, tutorial-pipeline-3 created.** Owner reviewed the map-and-retime approach (segment map + retime arithmetic in t-p-2 steps 125/162, autopsy in `docs/tts-screen-sync-brainstorm-handoff.md`) and rejected it — sentence-level sync ceiling, script shackled to the footage, untested flag rate. Instead the tutorial workflow adopts the same VO-first ordering as final-workflow (2026-07-12), refined same day in brainstorm: Claude writes a SECTIONED script from owner-supplied knowledge base (transcripts + dossier + vision) under a rulebook (no human reviewer — the rulebook + self-check is the script gate), marking each section `demo: yes/no` and flagging demo-specifics it can't know → tutorial maker resolves flags against the live tool → automated polish/lint → tutorial maker self-serve per-section TTS UI (locked voice, regen cap 3, respell box; tutorial maker LOCKS each take, text change after lock auto-invalidates its recording) → records one clip per demo section while listening (never an assembled video) → automated intake QC (duration/naming gate + filmstrip content gate) → visuals-flow owns ALL presentation (avatar-vs-graphics for non-demo sections via its existing shot pass, overlays, assembly on the VO timeline — script decides fact axis, visuals-flow decides look, shot/cue passes run in parallel with recording) → owner watches the draft render of early videos as backstop, then delegates. Concept doc: `pipelines/youtube/tutorial-pipeline-3/WORKFLOW.md` (no implementation yet). Pre-existing un-re-recordable footage gets polish-only dub via the legacy talk-over sync in `pipelines/video/tts/` or a re-record; no new sync automation will be built.
- 2026-07-21 (boss, owner) — **The dirty-main guard now AUTO-COMMITS instead of hard-refusing.** boss-dispatch had refused to dispatch whenever `REPO_ROOT` carried any uncommitted tracked change (greenlight parks such a land as "main checkout busy" and swallows a batch — the guard was added twice as docs then hardened to code after it bit the 044–050 batch). Owner directive: "make boss commit+push itself whenever such issues come" — the hand-clean-every-time friction wasn't worth the checkpoint for a single-user repo. New: `bin/boss-commit-main.sh [msg]` stages `git add -A` (honors .gitignore, so generated media — videos/*/screen.mp4, slices/, renders/, vo.mp3 — stays untracked and out of the commit, mirroring the video-workdir convention), commits with an auto area-summary message, and pushes. boss-dispatch calls it automatically when main is dirty; `BOSS_NO_AUTO_COMMIT=1` restores the old hard-refuse, `--force` skips the check entirely. session-start's warning now points at the helper. The guard's *purpose* (never let a dirty main silently park a merge) is preserved — it's just self-healing now instead of a stop.
- 2026-07-24 (visuals-flow, owner) — **visuals-flow v2 designed: a NEW pipeline folder (`pipelines/video/visuals-flow/`, v1 frozen as fallback once v2 is proven) that keeps the template/one-cue-pass/deterministic bet and ports Loop Studio's quality layers as machine-enforced rules.** Spec: `docs/specs/2026-07-24-visuals-flow-v2-design.md` (deltas A–I + a full Loop Studio coverage matrix). Owner decisions from the brainstorm: hybrid template-first (bespoke only when no card fits, each bespoke promoted into card-library — the flywheel toward templates-only); token-lean (~4 LLM calls/video); primary deliverable = layered FCPXML for DaVinci with sound/music/SFX as separate lanes + mastered preview mp4; longform 16:9 only (video.json keeps an aspect slot, nothing built for shorts); music = pipeline-downloaded royalty-free beds, owner swaps on the timeline later; ~12 enacted-device cards at launch; per-clause motion-density bar with always-on karaoke captions + persistent through-line motif; head layout modes (full/panel/hidden); sound stage places SFX from resolved.json's exact event times (no frame-diff detection); board becomes a two-tab dashboard (storyboard + Final Cut tab that subsumes Loop Studio's post-render reviewer: point-pinned notes, versions, live check-off, Gates A/B). REJECTED same session: basing v2 on Loop Studio's code (proprietary/keyed, Remotion stack, lacks cards/lint/board/FCPXML — ideas ported via the coverage matrix + reference-reading rule instead); re-opening Hyperframes-vs-Remotion (owner asked whether the Loop Studio look needs Remotion — no, same Chromium pixels; 2026-07-17 preview-IS-render rationale stands). Loop Studio study docs live at `pipelines/video/loop-studio/`. All execution via agy (owner, this session).
- 2026-07-24 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Two-stage review model: storyboard = quick HIGH-LEVEL pass, Final Cut = the real/final review.** Owner prefers reviewing the assembled draft (real motion/captions/SFX) over per-cue storyboard fragments; the storyboard tab remains the fast structural skim (placement, card choice, density — cue-keyed feedback) and the machine-gate surface (lint, audit gate, overflow, calibrate), while detailed taste feedback happens on the Final Cut tab (timestamped/pinned comments, versions, live check-off). Short videos may skip the human storyboard pass entirely (machine gates still run); long videos get a 60-second storyboard skim before paying a render+assemble round. Fix cost stays token-lean by design: cue/effects/sound edits are JSON + selective re-render + cached re-assemble, never a regeneration.
- 2026-07-24 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Review split refined + avatar mode parity with Loop Studio.** (1) Storyboard review scope = COMPOSITION ONLY: where screen recording, where which motion graphic, where the avatar and in which presentation mode. Effects + sound move entirely to Final Cut review — effects.json/sound.json stop being pre-render owner gates (auto-approved at generation; per-instance toggles remain for fixes; supersedes the per-artifact effects gate from 2026-07-20 for v2 — the owner now judges derivatives in motion, not from marker lanes). Storyboard tab leads with SCREEN/GRAPHICS/AVATAR lanes; effects/sound lanes collapsed by default. (2) Avatar presentation modes grow to Loop Studio parity: full, panel (floating PIP), corner bubble, hidden, and NEW `stage` — the head composited INTO a card's designed head_zone (their flagship head-in-scene treatment). Plans 145 (stage mode + head_zone card contract) and 146 (review-surface split).
- 2026-07-24 (visuals-flow, owner) — **DaVinci/Resolve export demoted to OPTIONAL escape hatch (final call deferred); the confirmed per-video review process:** (1) storyboard review — the time-investing pass: correct motion graphics + placement, avatar spans + presentation mode, add/delete, what stays raw screen recording; this approval also authorizes the HeyGen spend (avatar = the only expensive-to-change layer). (2) Session produces the cut unattended (render → auto-approved effects/sound → mix → 720p draft). (3) Final Cut review — timing feel, sound, effects; ≤2 rounds expected; owner approval triggers the full-res final.mp4 (the deliverable). `run.sh export` (layered FCPXML) remains available on demand — no longer a pipeline stage. Whether final.mp4 formally replaces the layered timeline as PRIMARY deliverable stays open ("will think later").
- 2026-07-25 (visuals-flow, owner) — **Avatar modes cut back to `full` + a new `side`; `stage` DELETED, `panel` and corner bubble parked unruled.** This reverses the "Loop Studio parity" mode set from 2026-07-24 (entry above) after the owner saw test-03 come out entirely full-screen. Two findings drove it. (1) The modes were enforced downstream but never taught upstream: the 070 prompt's Modes block was purely descriptive, no rule said when to pick a mode, `resolve-shots` silently defaulted `span.mode ?? 'full'`, and — decisively — prompt rule 5 ("NEVER place a span over a fullframe graphics cue") forbids exactly what `stage` REQUIRES while rule 7 ("on-screen-action stretches belong to the screen recording") forbids exactly what `panel` is for. The modes were unreachable by construction; test-03's spans carry no `mode` key at all because the Modes block did not exist at the commit its shot pass ran. (2) Owner judged `stage` (head inside a card's designed `head_zone`) the wrong shape — it needs every card redesigned with a hole in the right place (2 of 49 had one) and reads worse than a straight vertical split. `side` (motion graphics left 1200px, host right 720px, fixed right, hard edge, no radius/inset) replaces it and becomes the second main mode alongside `full`. Load-bearing implementation note: `stage`'s cover-crop geometry, ffmpeg mask/overlay chain and FCPXML crop+transform are exactly what `side` needs, so the deletion re-points tested maths at a constant zone rather than writing new. `mode` becomes REQUIRED (the silent default is the specific bug). `side` counts against `AVATAR_FULL_CAP` (it burns the same HeyGen seconds); `panel`'s pre-existing exemption is probably also wrong and was left alone to keep scope tight. `panel` + corner bubble stay code-live but rule-dead — reachable only by hand-editing `shots.json` — owner: "out of scope for now, will work on it in future". Owner constraint on the card side, verbatim: *"I don't want to suffer the quality of the motion graphics just because we have a side avatar"* — so no scaling a 1920 card into the column; a side-ready card must genuinely reflow at full type size, and cards that cannot (wide tables, matrices) declare `side: false` and are excluded by lint. `side` is a REQUIRED boolean on every fullframe catalog card so all 49 get a deliberate call. Plans 150 (pipeline), 151 (card contract + 21 reflowable cards), 152 (27 dense cards). Loop Studio parity now stands at 2 of their 4 layouts (`overlay`≈full, `pip`≈panel) plus `side`; their `split`/`stack` remain unbuilt, and `side` is closest to `split`.
